- **padz-datastore-persistence**: Explains the padz hybrid store architecture—files are truth, JSON metadata is a rebuildable cache. Use when working on storage, persistence, sync, doctor, or writing tests that need store simulation. ([arthur-debert/padz](https://github.com/arthur-debert/padz/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/padz-datastore-persistence))
- **padz-display-identifiers**: Explains the Display Identifier system in padz—a dual-identifier approach that provides stable, user-friendly IDs (1, 2, p1, d1) while internally using UUIDs. Use when working on pad listing, selection, or any CLI command that references pads by ID. Critical for understanding why filtered views preserve canonical indexes. ([arthur-debert/padz](https://github.com/arthur-debert/padz/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/padz-display-identifiers))
- **padz-for-agents**: Use padz to record, retrieve, and hand off notes and tasks across agent sessions. Trigger when: (1) the user asks to "save this as a note", "remember this for later", "turn this into a task / todo list"; (2) the agent needs to persist context across a compaction or handoff; (3) the user asks what's been noted, what's planned, what's done; (4) the repo already has a .padz/ directory and the user refers to "my notes" or "my pads". Not for working ON padz itself — see padz-output and padz-display-identifiers for that. ([arthur-debert/padz](https://github.com/arthur-debert/padz/tree/HEAD/docs/skills/padz-for-agents))
